([tyj-618/agent-skills](https://github.com/tyj-618/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-case-design))
- **eval-case-generation**: Generate new, high-value MongoDB Search / Vector Search / Hybrid Search eval cases for the Braintrust 'mongodb-mcp-server' suite — expert-voice prompts, stringified reference_answer ground truth, and 0–1.0 llm_judge rubrics. Use when authoring or scaling new dataset cases, writing scoring rubrics, or generating hybrid search / rerank ($rankFusion, $scoreFusion, $rerank) scenarios. ([nima-taheri-mongodb/search-eval-cases](https://github.com/nima-taheri-mongodb/search-eval-cases/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-case-generation))
- **eval-case-plan**: Writes evaluation cases for another agent, each with an input, an observable result, and a way to fail. Use when the user asks to test or benchmark an agent before calling the result a pass. ([brandononchain/agentpedia](https://github.com/brandononchain/agentpedia/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-ops/eval-case-plan))
- **eval-case-reporting**: Use when assistant, skill, prompt, hook, tool, or agent behavior is surprising, unsafe, brittle, partial, or worth preserving as an eval case and eval_case_reporting is enabled. ([jwilger/ai-plugins](https://github.com/jwilger/ai-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/development-system/skills/eval-case-reporting) · ★3)
- **eval-cases**: Turns acceptance criteria for a probabilistic behavior (a drafted text, an extracted value, a classification, an agent action) into eval cases: a golden set from real inputs, a scoring method, a threshold per risk tier agreed before the build, and never-cases with zero tolerance. Use when an item involves a language model or an agent, when a criterion says "accurate" or "works correctly", before marking an AI item Ready, and when a production failure must become a test. ([Benyoumen/backlog-writing-system](https://github.com/Benyoumen/backlog-writing-system/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-cases))
- **eval-changeset**: Evaluate a landed or in-flight changeset — one PR, or a whole stack squashed into a single integration branch — for complexity, whether that complexity was justified by the expressed intent, how cleanly each stacked PR incremented on its predecessors, whether any PR was too large to review or to go green on its own, how the system's design helped or fought the change, what it deferred into the `docs/dev/todo/` backlog instead of doing, and what redesign would have made it easier. Use when reviewing the cost of a change after the fact, deciding whether a stack was worth its size or split along… ([uppin/tddy-coder](https://github.com/uppin/tddy-coder/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-changeset) · ★1)
- **eval-chart-style**: Chart-type policy + house style for FAIL-vs-PASS LLM/VLM eval analysis figures. Use whenever you plot eval results inside an EvalRX analysis sandbox — matplotlib PNGs under figures/ or deterministic chart specs — and the point is to NOT default to a bar chart. This skill decides WHAT chart to draw and the semantic palette; for journal-grade polish (fonts, export, QA) additionally apply the nature-figure skill on the figures you draw. ([evalvitals/evalrx](https://github.com/evalvitals/evalrx/tree/HEAD/evalrx/agent_assets/skills/eval-chart-style) · ★18)
- **eval-charter**: Replay a Bot's last N runs against its own charter never-list and report every breach. Use when asked whether a Bot is behaving, before widening its permissions, or on a weekly system eval. Read-only; it audits run history and never edits the Bot, its charter, or its routine. ([JYeswak/grok_bot_playground](https://github.com/JYeswak/grok_bot_playground/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/eval-charter) · ★1)
- **eval-charts**: Generates LLM and agent benchmark evaluation charts and token/tool usage summaries (`token_usage_summary.csv`, `token_usage_summary.json`) from `results.jsonl` benchmark runs using the `altair-charts` skill. Use this skill when the user wants to analyze, summarize, or plot agent/model benchmark evaluation results across tasks (`case_id`), models, and effort tiers (`low`, `medium`, `high`), including stacked token breakdowns (`Cached Input`, `Input`, `Output`), stacked tool invocation breakdowns, cost (`cost_usd`), duration (`duration_sec`), turns (`num_turns`), or pass rates (`pass_rate`). ([danistrebel/agy-effort-benchmarks](https://github.com/danistrebel/agy-effort-benchmarks/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-charts) · ★3)
- **eval-chat-traces**: Systematically review AI chat traces to find failure patterns using error analysis methodology (inspired by Hamel Husain's evals framework). ([geman220/ECS-Discord-Bot](https://github.com/geman220/ECS-Discord-Bot/tree/HEAD/.kiro/skills/eval-chat-traces) · ★2)
- **eval-chatbot**: Run, interpret and extend the AI assistant's eval suite. Use after changing the system prompt, tools, model or content, when a bad chatbot answer is reported, or when asked to evaluate/test the chatbot. ([lintenn/my-portfolio](https://github.com/lintenn/my-portfolio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-chatbot) · ★1)
- **eval-check**: Evaluate the full harness configuration as a system. Scans all skills, commands, CLAUDE.md, and hooks for redundancy, overlap, type misclassification, and structural issues. Produces an informational report with restructuring suggestions. Use when the user wants to check their overall setup health, find redundant skills, detect overlapping triggers, or get restructuring recommendations before diving into individual skill evaluation. Triggers on "check my setup", "harness health", "are my skills redundant", "what should I merge", "setup overview", "configuration check". ([fullsend-ai/agent-eval-harness](https://github.com/fullsend-ai/agent-eval-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-check) · ★50 · 2 copies)
- **eval-check**: Check a language-model evaluation claim or an eval run for the ways eval numbers go wrong - a benchmark score in a paper, model card or leaderboard, a claim that model A beats model B, a judge-model result, or the user's own eval harness and results files. Use when someone asks whether an LLM benchmark number holds up, wants to compare models fairly, is writing up eval results, or is building or debugging an eval harness or scorer. ([Heuremen-LLC/indahl.ai](https://github.com/Heuremen-LLC/indahl.ai/tree/HEAD/claude-plugins/openai/indahl-eval-check/skills/eval-check) · 33 copies)
- **eval-check**: Run agent quality evaluations when core agent code changes. Runs fast evals automatically, asks before expensive LLM evals. ([rosinbum/usopc-athlete-support-agent](https://github.com/rosinbum/usopc-athlete-support-agent/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-check))
- **eval-checkpoint**: Evaluate a MarinFold contacts-v1 checkpoint on the FoldBench monomer eval sets (eval-val for routine work, eval-test as a rarely-read held-out set, eval-denovo for designs; exp245) or the legacy exp89 554-protein benchmark, scored with exp82's rollout+resample recipe. Use for checkpoint scoring, R-precision/AUC requests, comparisons with structure baselines, held-out or decontaminated-accuracy claims, designed-vs-natural or viral splits, sequence-KNN nulls, or reproducing contact metrics on local, CUDA, CoreWeave GPU or Iris TPU execution. ([Open-Athena/MarinFold](https://github.com/Open-Athena/MarinFold/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/eval-checkpoint) · ★35)
- **eval-checkpoint**: Evaluate a nano checkpoint and pick the right diagnostic for the question. Use this skill when the user wants to evaluate a checkpoint, score sample quality (CLAP / spectral / silence / onset), check overfitting (train vs val), decide whether lyric conditioning is worth the compute, sweep sampling parameters, check genre coverage, or sanity-check DAC codec fidelity. ([diskrotrepo/nano](https://github.com/diskrotrepo/nano/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-checkpoint) · ★4)
- **eval-checkpoint**: Runs structured evaluation passes over agent output artifacts. Supports checkpoint and continuous modes. Computes weighted pass@k scores across grader suites, triggers adversarial red-team when stakes are high, and gates pipeline progression — routing failures back upstream with structured fix instructions before a defect can propagate. ([Tripnri/tripnri](https://github.com/Tripnri/tripnri/tree/HEAD/.claude/eval-checkpoint))
- **eval-ci**: Puts a Claude Agent SDK project's eval suite behind a CI gate that means something, and says where the project's evaluation practice actually stands. Generates two GitHub Actions workflows that run the runner copy committed in the project as a plain step, so the exit code is the verdict and no agent decides what runs: a pull-request tier with free graders, one run per case, a cost ceiling and cancel-superseded concurrency, and a nightly tier with judge graders and repeated runs. Wires the credential as a repository secret (CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN from `claude setup-token` by default, the ANTH… ([alexaiarchitect/claude-agent-sdk-skills](https://github.com/alexaiarchitect/claude-agent-sdk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-ci))
- **eval-ci**: Generates cost-tiered GitHub Actions workflows for Google ADK evaluations and assesses where a project's evaluation practice stands. Produces a PR-tier workflow that runs only deterministic metrics and gates on a real exit code, and a nightly tier for judge metrics, groundedness, safety and repeated runs with a reliability report; wires Workload Identity to Vertex AI or an API-key secret; uploads eval history as an artifact; and lints existing workflows for the mistake that matters most, running bare `adk eval`, which exits 0 whatever happened through ADK 2.12.0 and, from 2.13.0, exits 1 on a… ([alexaiarchitect/google-adk-skills](https://github.com/alexaiarchitect/google-adk-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/eval-ci))
- **eval-ci-new**: Promote labeled traces into evals/golden/, generate a GitHub Actions eval-gate workflow (pinned model, code evals + validated judges, regression threshold), and install the test-split-locking hook. - **eval-citation-validator**: Validate that clinical claims contain complete source citation metadata. ([code-qtzl/openemr-base-clean](https://github.com/code-qtzl/openemr-base-clean/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/eval-citation-validator))
- **eval-cluster-failures**: Use when several runs across a cohort came out wrong and you must tell whether they share one cause or have several. Tells you which trace features to cluster on, why clustering on the wrong answer's value misleads, how many cases a cluster needs before it is worth reporting, and how to name a cluster so that a fix can be aimed at it. Does not decide which runs were wrong - ask the deterministic scorer for the list. ([YeechingTiger/agentic-chart-review-with-semantica](https://github.com/YeechingTiger/agentic-chart-review-with-semantica/tree/HEAD/assets/skills/eval-cluster-failures))

- **eval-drift**: Per-case regression detection for `claude plugin eval`. The built-in eval scores a run but does not remember the previous one, so a case that passed yesterday and fails today is invisible unless the suite average happens to dip — and a change that fixes two cases while breaking one lifts the average and ships the break. This records each run's per-case outcome as a baseline and compares the next run against it case by case, blocking on any pass-to-fail flip regardless of the average. Also flags flaky cases and still-passing cases that got less reliable.
